    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)

    FROM [BatchProcessLogs] AS [b]

) AS [t] ON [p].[OrderID] = [t].[OrderId]

SELECT [p].[BatchID], [p].[BatchNumber], [p].[OrderID], CASE
    WHEN [p1].[OrderID] IS NOT NULL THEN [p1].[OrderCode]
    ELSE ''
END, CASE
    WHEN [p1].[OrderID] IS NOT NULL AND [r].[RecipeID] IS NOT NULL THEN [r].[RecipeName]
    ELSE N'Sản phẩm'
END, [p].[Status], [p1].[OrderID], [r].[RecipeID], [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialId], [t].[RequiredQuantity], [t].[DispensingStatus], [t].[MaterialName], [t].[MaterialCode], [t].[UomName], [t].[MaterialID0], [t].[UomID], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p3]
        WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p3].[BatchId]) AND NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p4]
        WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p4].[BatchId] AND [p4].[DispensingStatus] <> N'Dispensed') THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END
FROM [ProductionBatches] AS [p]
LEFT JOIN [ProductionOrders] AS [p1] ON [p].[OrderID] = [p1].[OrderID]
LEFT JOIN [Recipes] AS [r] ON [p1].[RecipeID] = [r].[RecipeID]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p2].[OrderBomId], [p2].[MaterialId], [p2].[RequiredQuantity], [p2].[DispensingStatus], CASE
        WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m].[MaterialName]
        ELSE N'Unknown'
    END AS [MaterialName], CASE
        WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m].[MaterialCode]
        ELSE ''
    END AS [MaterialCode], CASE
        WHEN [u].[UomID] IS NOT NULL THEN [u].[UomName]
        ELSE N'N/A'
    END AS [UomName], [m].[MaterialID] AS [MaterialID0], [u].[UomID], [p2].[BatchId]
    FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p2]
    LEFT JOIN [Materials] AS [m] ON [p2].[MaterialId] = [m].[MaterialID]
    LEFT JOIN [UnitOfMeasure] AS [u] ON [p2].[UomId] = [u].[UomID]
) AS [t] ON [p].[BatchID] = [t].[BatchId]
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p0]
    WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p0].[BatchId])
ORDER BY [p].[BatchID], [p1].[OrderID], [r].[RecipeID], [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialID0]

SELECT [p].[BatchID], [p].[BatchNumber], [p].[OrderID], CASE
    WHEN [p1].[OrderID] IS NOT NULL THEN [p1].[OrderCode]
    ELSE ''
END, CASE
    WHEN [p1].[OrderID] IS NOT NULL AND [r].[RecipeID] IS NOT NULL THEN [r].[RecipeName]
    ELSE N'Sản phẩm'
END, [p].[Status], [p1].[OrderID], [r].[RecipeID], [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialId], [t].[RequiredQuantity], [t].[DispensingStatus], [t].[MaterialName], [t].[MaterialCode], [t].[UomName], [t].[MaterialID0], [t].[UomID], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p3]
        WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p3].[BatchId]) AND NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p4]
        WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p4].[BatchId] AND [p4].[DispensingStatus] <> N'Dispensed') THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END
FROM [ProductionBatches] AS [p]
LEFT JOIN [ProductionOrders] AS [p1] ON [p].[OrderID] = [p1].[OrderID]
LEFT JOIN [Recipes] AS [r] ON [p1].[RecipeID] = [r].[RecipeID]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p2].[OrderBomId], [p2].[MaterialId], [p2].[RequiredQuantity], [p2].[DispensingStatus], CASE
        WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m].[MaterialName]
        ELSE N'Unknown'
    END AS [MaterialName], CASE
        WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m].[MaterialCode]
        ELSE ''
    END AS [MaterialCode], CASE
        WHEN [u].[UomID] IS NOT NULL THEN [u].[UomName]
        ELSE N'N/A'
    END AS [UomName], [m].[MaterialID] AS [MaterialID0], [u].[UomID], [p2].[BatchId]
    FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p2]
    LEFT JOIN [Materials] AS [m] ON [p2].[MaterialId] = [m].[MaterialID]
    LEFT JOIN [UnitOfMeasure] AS [u] ON [p2].[UomId] = [u].[UomID]
) AS [t] ON [p].[BatchID] = [t].[BatchId]
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p0]
    WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p0].[BatchId])
ORDER BY [p].[BatchID], [p1].[OrderID], [r].[RecipeID], [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialID0]
